# ORDER ui-v3-t9r2 · C0 AUDIT — inventory appendix

Totals across the 13 areas: exists 289 · partial 156 · broken 46 · missing 46.

Every screen, key and data source, per reader; each reader's "broken" claims were re-verified by an independent skeptic (verdicts shown). Paths are tree-relative: BASE = origin/main 01cd3a7 + PR #64 (231b0f4), UINEXT2 = origin/order/ui-next-2 a92c949 (PR #47 on #46), C6 = origin/order/ui-cockpit-k7m3-c6 386f99d (PR #59). Generated from the reader results; hashes truncated.

## SCREENS

BASE has one v2 shell (ShellV2.tsx, 1637 lines) with 6 tabs (HOME RUN CHAIN LIBRARY CHAT COMMAND; digits 1-6), 20 pane functions, 9 rendered overlays, a boot frame from boot.cjs, and a bounded-rows solver whose contract (tests/bounded-rows.test.tsx) passes 6/6 in ~43s: 14 shot labels x 2 grids = the "28 screens", but 2 labels are no-op duplicates (library-demos presses D, chain-open presses Enter — neither does anything in BASE), so 12 distinct screens are pinned. Not in the contract: qr/sealconfirm/refuse/newrun/harnesspick/note overlays, the CHAT drawer, INSERT mode, the boot frame, the v1 Onboarding, wizard/DEMOS (UINEXT2-only). HANDS and ENGINE ROOM are not tabs (COMMAND sub-tab / rail card; ENGINE ROOM is invisible at 80 cols). The COMMAND pane advertises 9 keys (m M K t b f g H, 1-6 focus) that have no handler; [M] opens an invisible modal. UINEXT2 adds DEMOS/SIGNAL/OLLAMA/IntegrationStatus/wizard/studio.preserve but has no row solver (HOME at 80 cols renders 32 rows; which-key is fixed 100 wide). C6 adds an unbounded 8-hand HANDS pane. No screen shows run id or arsenal debt; no judgment/prediction typed views exist for the prediction kinds actually on the chain. Fresh-install captures show zero PII pattern classes, but the UINEXT2 wizard's first screen prints an absolute cwd path. Legacy v1 (views/layout/panels/Onboarding) is still the first-run mount path and teaches keys the v2 shell does not have.

**Open questions**

- Which entry is canonical for the ORDER's 'first screen': boot.cjs + fast-entry (direct ShellV2 at stdout.columns) or cli.tsx → shell-entry → startTUI/App (ShellV2 inside Layout at width W-2 after Onboarding)? The frame contract covers only the direct mount.
- Does order/blank-slate-v1k9's `timmy init` (PR #48) replace or duplicate UINEXT2's wizard.tsx? Only UINEXT2's was read here; both would need one home for C7.
- Is the ORDER's 'hands.json' a rename of cockpit.ts board.json, or a second file? Every existing test and verb reads board.json.
- Which of the eight demo routes map to lanes/demos/families.json's nine families (card-film, house, cad-fabrication, courier, material-effects, vector-motion, unreal-observatory, grand-canyon, signal)? None is named 'ask the scene', 'refuse', 'eight hands', 'materialize', 'blank slate' or 'factory day'.
- Should the 'arsenal debt' number on the status line come from lanes/vision/checkpoints/C0.json (order/vision-v7r3) or from a new timmy arsenal verb? Neither exists on BASE.
- Is `TIMMY_SHELL=v1` (views.tsx/layout.tsx/panels/*, 15 panels, panel-audit + tui-ergonomics + keyboard-contract.pty tests) to be deleted under this ORDER, or kept one more release as tests/cutover.test.tsx states?
- Why do ck5-home-fresh captures show 'chain ✓ 1' and a model id on a fresh store — is the FIX 3 model.policy seed (ShellV2.tsx:229-239) compatible with C7's 'HOME empty'?

## KEYS

The v2 keyboard is one pure reducer (BASE src/tui/shell-mode.ts) + a per-(mode,tab) keymap (src/tui/keymap.ts) + an effect loop in ShellV2.tsx. It is 6 tabs (1-6), j/k, Enter, Tab, /, i/:, c, ?, Esc, s=seal-confirm everywhere, r=refuse on RUN, q=QR on HOME, plus COMMAND swarm/hands verbs. Tests: tui-keyboard 6/6 pass, shell-backspace 2/2 pass, keyboard-contract.pty 2 skipped (gated on TIMMY_PTY_TESTS=1 + tmux; it asserts v1 markers and quits with q, so it cannot pass against the v2 default even if ungated). The reducer emits 12 actions nobody consumes (focus-pane:N, cmd-model/body/fusion/generate/toggle/handoff/harness-set/model-set, open-files, act:seal review note, hands-move/prompt are state-only), the M overlay is invisible and traps keys, the S status overlay is sticky, and the footer/which-key advertise digits-as-tabs on COMMAND, [m] model, [f] files, [Enter] open on HOME/CHAIN/COMMAND, and [s] seal on CHAT, none of which do what they say. Nothing in any tree implements the ORDER's contract: 8 tabs, s=send, r=replay, a status line with run id/cost/arsenal debt/last seal, or a q that quits. UINEXT2 adds D/[/]/Enter demo keys (unlisted in footer/which-key) and the wizard's e/w/j/k/q; C6 adds nothing beyond BASE's hands keys and regresses which-key to a fixed 100 cols in-flow.

**Open questions**

- Does the ORDER intend HANDS and ENGINE ROOM to become real tabs (keys 6/7) or keep the COMMAND sub-tab/rail? Today h (COMMAND) vs h (LIBRARY harness) and w/l/X/H/T/S/U/J/P swarm verbs all live on the same tab and would have to move.
- Where does refuse go if r = replay? C3 keeps REFUSE rows with reason; the reducer's refuse overlay (shell-mode.ts:51-62) is the only reason-capture UI.
- Where does seal go if s = send? owner.seal is the only human-gated seal key in the shell (shell-mode.ts:170, ShellV2.tsx:459).
- Is the CHAT tab meant to keep 'any printable enters chat' (shell-mode.ts:149)? Under that rule the shared contract keys (j/k, ?, s, r, digits) cannot exist on CHAT; digits 1-6 are the sole exception today.
- Should the PTY keyboard-contract test (v1 markers) be rewritten for v2 or retired? It is the only test named 'keyboard-contract' and CI excludes it.
- Which tree is the rebase target for keys: BASE (hands + absolute which-key) or UINEXT2/C6 (demos + wizard, which-key regressed)? The three disagree on shell-mode.ts and ShellChrome.tsx and no tree has all of D/[/], hands, and the C5 which-key fix.
- When ShellV2 runs under the App path (views.tsx) the root dispatcher also handles Tab and ^C; is the shell-entry path (ink default ^C) the only intended launcher post-cutover?

## DATA SOURCES

The v2 shell reads ~35 distinct sources: the receipt chain (store-pin resolved), the bus tail of runs.jsonl, .timmy/model-policy.json, .timmy/escrow, committed shelves (lanes/swarm/{presets,runs}, lanes/sandbox/runs, lanes/abilities/results, src/models/{cache,notes}.json, orders.log), home-dir trees (~/timmy/{drop,projects}, sibling folders of cwd), and probes (docker, tmux timmy-war, `command -v` for 12 harness binaries, commander WebSocket, fit.mjs spawns). UINEXT2 adds lanes/demos/families.json, lanes/engines/*.json, lanes/schema/model-strictness.json, the-signal checkpoint files and a wizard that reads/writes .timmy/private/config.json. Every reader degrades to an inert dash/empty line when its file is absent; nothing crashes. Two things are demonstrably wrong: (1) the bus path ignores the store-pin, so seals land on the pinned chain while their bus events land in cwd — verified on three checkouts; (2) the first render of ShellV2 WRITES (.timmy/model-policy.json + a model.policy seal) — verified: two such seals were appended to the shared pinned chain during this audit. Against the ORDER: no reader exists for demo.<name> seals (DEMOS fills from a committed JSON with null seals), hands.json vs board.json, cell states/[s] send/needs-approval only on the dropped C4 commit, no arsenal-debt or run-id reader, engines rows only for Houdini/Unreal on UINEXT2, typed CHAIN views are hand-listed (10/15 vs 137 kinds on the chain), and the UINEXT2 wizard prints an absolute store path on a first-run screen.

**Open questions**

- Who rendered ShellV2 in the audit worktrees at 22:37 local (05:37Z)? Both audit-base and audit-uinext2 now carry .timmy/model-policy.json, and the shared pinned chain gained two 'model.policy · default …' seals plus two more sealed lines (unreal.render, unreal.passes) since the brief's 5259 — the C0 report must decide whether these count as incident evidence under §14 (they are sealed and cannot …
- hands.json (ORDER) vs board.json (code): rename, alias, or a new file with live cell states beside the imported board?
- Should readers honor one root? TIMMY_REPO_ROOT is respected by warroom2/cockpit/uinext but not by receipts.ts, bus/index.ts, policy.ts or overlay.mjs (module-relative ROOT) — fixture captures currently need TIMMY_REPO_ROOT and TIMMY_STORE together to stay read-only.
- Are the frv2/ck5 manifests signed (ed25519 like receipts) as C8 requires? Not verified in this area — the manifest files exist and the ui.captures seal exists.
- fleet/nodes.json: the operator's checkout has a repo-root copy (gitignored) but no .timmy/private/fleet/nodes.json overlay; which convention is law for the ENGINE ROOM reader?
- The receipt chain shows demo.ask-the-scene.preflight (route/castRecorded/replayVerified) sealed on 2026-09-14 — is that the intended shape for demo.<name> seals the DEMOS rows should read, or is demo.cast (cast_sha256) the shape?

## STATUS LINE · RUN ID · CHAIN

Header today (BASE, identical in UINEXT2/C6): "TIMMY" + 6 tab digits/labels + segments [chain ✓ count · head8] [bus ●/○] [drops n] [model x | "nothing needs you"], dropping right-to-left; at 80 tabs collapse to digits and only the chain segment survives. None of the ORDER's four status fields exist: no run id (RunRow.id is the subject's second token, never rendered; CHAIN rows have no run column; runIdOf reads sources[0].run_id and only swarm.* receipts carry it — 47 of 3180 bodies on the pinned chain), no cost (cost exists as HOME "cost today", COMMAND "spend", chat.turn cost_usd; escrow ceiling/drawn), no arsenal debt anywhere in any tree (only vision-v7r3 C0.json block + doc verb spec), "last seal" only as head hash in the chain segment and per-hand SEAL in HANDS. CHAIN typed views are a hand-written switch on exact subject: 10 cases in BASE, +5 exact +2 prefix in UINEXT2 (C6 unchanged); they cover 61/3180 (BASE) or 64/3180 (UINEXT2) receipt bodies on the pinned chain; 1310 distinct subjects exist. UINEXT2's signal.* prefix keys on subject, but all 73 signal.* receipts have kind signal.* and subject "The Signal …", so none hit. Prediction/measured/difference rides on 8 spline.checkpoint + hana.c1 receipts but no TUI computes or renders a difference; DEMOS shows PRED/EV seal ids only. Refusals are red (theme.danger #FF5D75, not tokens.json REFUSE #FF3B3B) via status denied/failed, but escrow refusal seals status "ok" with the reason in discrepancies, which nothing renders. Five requested test files: 22/22 pass in BASE.

**Open questions**

- Which id is 'the run id' for non-swarm receipts: the hermes receipt schema run_id (src/receipt/schema.ts:40), the runtime runId (src/runtime/types.ts), the cockpit marker, or a new field sealed on every receipt? 1368 of 3180 bodies on the pinned chain have no status and most have no run_id.
- Should escrow refusal seal status 'denied' (breaking the current 'transition receipts are always ok' shape and the chain-pane/run-pane fixtures) or should the TUI read discrepancies/decisions[].reason to render the reason?
- Should typed views key on kind (as the chain's signal.*/film.step.*/spatial.* families do) or on subject (as the current switch does), and is a chain census at boot acceptable as the 'listed from the chain' source?
- Is 'cost' per run (sum of cost_usd for the selected run's group), per day (costToday), or the commander/governor spend? The three disagree today.
- Is 'last seal' the head of runs.jsonl (current), the newest receipt.sealed event (v1), or the last seal of the current run?
- Which theme red is law for the TUI: theme.danger #FF5D75 or tokens.json REFUSE #FF3B3B (both token files agree on #FF3B3B)?

## DEMOS · CAST · FILM FRAMES

C4 is essentially unbuilt against the ORDER. `timmy demo` exists (BASE src/cli.ts:611 → src/demo/cast.ts) but has no run/list subcommands: any trailing word is ignored and it always records the one scripted war-room cast (HOME → RUN → COMMAND/SWARM → launch → CHAIN swarm.airgap → [o]), 6 frames, 5.4 s of timestamps (~6.8 s with hold), 120x32, frozen clock + seeded PRNG. Byte-identity is real but only same-machine/same-commit (tests/demo-cast.test.ts passes locally in 37 s with agg; CI excludes it for lack of agg). The demo.cast seal carries cast+mp4 sha only, cites no receipts, and six of its seven chain instances share id rc_mjuohs00_annv with ts 2026-01-01 (frozen clock never restored). The UINEXT2 DEMOS card fills from lanes/demos/families.json + a `fill` subject prefix, not from demo.<name> seals; no STALE/replay check, no in-pane [r] ([r] is REFUSE on RUN); the picker walks 9 rows while only 4 render. C6 adds `timmy cockpit shot` (fixture board, 120/80 captures, cast+gif+mp4, manifest, cockpit.shot seal, tests 5/5) — the strongest existing pattern for C4/C8. C8: frv2 captures exist for the five moments under one marker frv2-a92c949, hashed in an unsigned manifest, sealed as ui.captures rc_mty4zsmo_0w9x; but frames carry boot residue lines, the 80-col prediction/judgment frames are one CHAIN row, the reuse-120 frame lost its header, no reduced-motion variants, and no 20-second cast of any real route exists.

**Open questions**

- Which script produced the frv2-* frames and the eight manifest-frv2-<commit>.json files? None is committed on any inspected ref; without it C8 cannot be re-taken deterministically.
- Does the ORDER intend demo.<name> to be a new seal subject family (e.g. demo.eight-hands) or to rename the existing demo.cast / cockpit.shot shapes? The chain currently has demo.code, demo.browse, demo.cast, demo.ask-the-scene.preflight and cockpit.shot with four different source shapes.
- order demo-d6t2 (seal rc_mu0r538q_9y77, untracked studio/demo-d6t2/) is already working route 1 'ask the scene' with castRecorded/replayVerified flags — is ui-v3-t9r2 C4 meant to consume its seals, or is it a parallel effort? (Directory not read per audit rules.)
- The ORDER's C2 keyboard contract assigns 'r' to replay while the v2 shell uses 'r' for REFUSE on RUN (shell-mode.ts:183) — which wins?
- The demo.cast seal with a real timestamp (rc_mjuohs00_tdrg, 2026-09-09) records mp4:'none' while the six frozen-ts seals record mp4 hashes — should the six frozen-clock seals be superseded by a keep-assertion seal (§13) before DEMOS rows are allowed to fill from demo.* seals?
- tests/demo-cast.test.ts is WIP on CI only for lack of `agg` — is installing agg in the workflow in scope for C4, or should the byte-identity gate compare cast text only (drop the mp4 equality) so CI can run it?
- Do the eight ORDER routes map onto the families in lanes/demos/families.json (9 portfolio families) or replace them? 'render a world' ≈ unreal-observatory/grand-canyon, 'factory day' ≈ signal, but ask-the-scene, predict-build-judge, refuse, materialize, blank slate have no family row.
- Should the cockpit-shot pattern (throwaway root + gate + manifest + cockpit.shot seal), currently on PR #59 stacked on #47, be generalized into `timmy demo run <route>` on main, given the ORDER says branch AFTER #47 and the cockpit land?

## HANDS

HANDS in BASE is a COMMAND sub-tab ([h]) fed by `.timmy/private/cockpit/board.json`, imported from a ROUNDS.md chart by `timmy cockpit board import`; it renders hands × fixed R0–R4 with a cursor, prompt viewer and C5 row budget; tests 5/5 green (C6: 10/10). Against the ORDER's C5 line: rows come from board.json, not hands.json — hands.json is a different file on order/factory-f1d0 (#54): a launch registry (name/kind/cli/worktree) with no rounds, states or prompts, and #54's `timmy cockpit` verb set and `lanes/cockpit/` directory collide with BASE's (BASE's leak-check flags `lanes/cockpit/*`, verified). Columns are a hard-coded constant, not derived. All five cell states exist in the type and render (STOP red, needs-approval amber, HOLD seal-green, running cyan, idle muted), but the only classifier that produces them from pane logs (C4 INGEST, commit 6a5c9aa, `hand.report` seals behind the privacy gate) was dropped from PR #64 and is absent from BASE and C6, although its `ui.cockpit.c4` seal and "panes ingested" C5 captures remain on the chain/disk. `timmy cockpit send` and `hand.prompt` exist nowhere; `s` on a HANDS cell opens the global seal-confirm and Enter appends an `owner.seal · via HOME [s]` receipt. The board-exists guard and fresh-install test are present; the live pane has no privacy gate (home-path worktree renders, verified) and rows carry the order id, never a run id.

The failure is a predicted merge conflict, verified at the function level, not yet observed on a real combined tree. |

**Open questions**

- Which file is the source of truth for HANDS rows: the ORDER's hands.json (factory-f1d0's launch registry, no rounds/states/prompts) or the ROUNDS.md-imported board.json that carries them? Both live in .timmy/private/cockpit/.
- Which pane-log convention survives — factory's pipe-pane `.timmy/private/cockpit/<hand>/<date>.log` or the dropped ingest's `panes/<hand>.log` (`timmy cockpit pane-path`)?
- Was C4 INGEST (6a5c9aa) dropped from PR #64 deliberately? Its ui.cockpit.c4 seal and the ck4/ck5 'panes ingested' captures remain; is C4 to be re-cut onto BASE before C5 of this ORDER?
- How should `timmy cockpit` resolve between #54's verbs (hands/up/attach/status/down via lanes/cockpit/cockpit.mjs) and BASE's (board import/show/leak-check, C6 shot), given they occupy the same `if (command === 'cockpit')` branch and BASE's leak-check flags #54's lanes/cockpit/ directory?
- Does the ORDER intend [s] to override the global seal key only while the HANDS cursor is on a cell, and what should the confirm overlay say so a SEND is never mistaken for a seal?
- Under the C1 tokens law, which hue is HOLD (not sealed, so not SEAL green) and which is running (cyan is not admitted), and which evidence state does a cell carry (chart-declared vs pane-checked vs stale)?
- Where did the ck6 cockpit.shot captures land (not in UI2 .timmy/captures), and should the frozen-clock seal rc_mjuohs00_ks6k be superseded or annotated on the chain?
- At 80x24 with eight hands, is the C5 cursor window (2–3 hands visible with a '<n> more hands' overflow line) acceptable for 'all eight hands on screen', or must the film shot be 120x40 only?

## ENGINE ROOM · LIBRARY

Neither tree has an ENGINE ROOM or LIBRARY that matches C6. In BASE, ENGINE ROOM is one card in the COMMAND right rail (lowest fold priority, absent at 80x24) whose only rows are fleet/nodes.json nodes fed by node.* receipts — the file is a gitignored private overlay (absent from every public tree, so the card renders "fleet/nodes.json missing") and the chain carries 0 node.join receipts, so Spark rows could only ever read "down". UINEXT2 adds a Houdini line (renders; reads lanes/engines/*.json) and an Unreal line gated on a lanes/unreal directory that exists only on order/unreal-m3r8, so it never renders in UINEXT2 or BASE. Spline, Hana and Omma rows exist nowhere; their feeds live on unmerged branches (lanes/visual on showpiece/hana, lanes/factory on factory-f1d0) plus spline.*/hana.*/omma.* receipts on the pinned chain. "OLLAMA LOCAL/CLOUD" (UINEXT2 LIBRARY rail) labels six OpenRouter models from lanes/schema/model-strictness.json as "cloud"; "schema" there is tool-schema strictness — no JSON-schema/structured-output notion exists for Ollama anywhere. LIBRARY in BASE = MODELS/BOARDS/PROJECTS + FLEET/SKILLS rails; only MODELS scrolls (j/k), FLEET/SKILLS show an overflow line only. "Families" exist only as UINEXT2's DEMOS card (lanes/demos/families.json, 7 rows, all seals null). Not one of the five evidence states is rendered anywhere in src/tui of BASE, UINEXT2 or C6; theme.ts has no GENERATED/PREDICT tokens. UINEXT2 lacks the C5 bounded-rows contract: its LIBRARY frame rendered 78 lines at 120x40. tests: warroom2 6/6 and library-pane 1/1 pass in BASE.

**Open questions**

- Does the ORDER's 'Sparks' row mean the DGX Spark nodes of fleet/nodes.json (spark1/2/3 in the example registry)? Assumed yes; if it means something else, nothing in src or lanes matches.
- What does 'schema' mean for the Ollama row — the captain-y9g4 tool-schema strict\|lenient table (OpenRouter-only today, 0 Ollama rows) or per-model structured-output / JSON-schema support? No code measures either for Ollama models; the only Ollama read is /api/tags names.
- Should the ENGINE ROOM Unreal/Houdini rows be fed from lanes/engines/inventory.json (on main, all 8 engines with installed/proven) rather than from a lanes/unreal directory that lives only on order/unreal-m3r8?
- For Spline/Hana/Omma: feed the rows from the branch artifacts (lanes/visual, lanes/factory — not on main) or from the pinned chain's spline.*/hana.*/omma.* receipts, which exist now?
- Is PROJECTS (BoardsPane) listing the names of sibling directories of the checkout acceptable under 'no personal data on any first screen'? It prints operator folder names on LIBRARY.
- Why is fleet/nodes.json a private overlay while ENGINE ROOM depends on it — should a fresh install show the Sparks row as 'declared' (example registry) rather than 'fleet/nodes.json missing'?
- The UINEXT2 ADAPTERS line (HOME) and the vision C0 arsenal_debt block overlap in intent — which one is the arsenal-debt source of record for the status line?

## FIRST RUN

Two unrelated `timmy init` implementations exist and neither is on main. UINEXT2 (PR #47) has a 5-step ink wizard (store / operator / edge host / commander ws / model policy) that writes only .timmy/private/config.json, is reachable only by typing `timmy init` (not listed in help, no first-run gate, no shell key), and its "model policy" step writes an overlay key nothing reads. order/blank-slate-v1k9 (PR #48) has a readline wizard with exactly the ORDER's four steps (operator, seed, providers, first project), a path guard proving writes land only under ~/timmy, .timmy/private and the gitignored .timmy/store-pin, a first-run gate (blank-slate-guard as cli.tsx's first import + no-arg cli.ts), `timmy release check` (docker node:24-bookworm + inside.mjs with the §12 negative control), hashed identity terms, orders.log moved private; tests 5/5 and tsc clean in a scratch git-archive. A three-way merge of the two cli.ts files produces 2 conflict hunks (the no-arg/help gate and the `init` verb block) — they will collide. Release check is NOT green: both release.check seals on the chain (rc_mty1t4qp_ohus, rc_mty22hgk_7yxt) carry ok=false with tree_personal=138 (studio casts), and lanes/release/check.mjs is absent from main. On BASE a fresh boot goes cli.tsx → shell-entry → v1 Onboarding (config.onboarded unset), so the first screen is the v1 wordmark splash, not HOME; ShellV2 seals `model.policy · default` on first mount (writes .timmy/receipts + .timmy/model-policy.json), and onboarding writes the Conf user-config store (API key), .env and .timmy/logorg.json — all outside ~/timmy and .timmy/private. The "nothing sealed yet" HOME line exists only on UINEXT2/C6; the ck5-home-fresh captures predate the #64 rebase and do not represent BASE.

**Open questions**

- Which `timmy init` is the ORDER's keep-and-improve base: the ink WizardScreen (#47, wrong steps, ShellV2 look) or blank-slate's runInit (#48, right steps, readline, plus guard/release check)? The ORDER says branch after #47 lands but names C7 steps that only #48 has.
- Should the first-mount `model.policy · default` seal (ShellV2.tsx:242, both trees) be suppressed on a blank slate so HOME is empty and release-check's receipts_after_launch can pass?
- Does the v1 Onboarding gate (config.onboarded in the Conf user-config store, with the API key stored there in plaintext) get removed, or does the C7 wizard set it — otherwise a fresh ShellV2 install still opens on the v1 splash.
- The UINEXT2 fresh captures show chain ✓ 0 although UINEXT2 ShellV2.tsx:244 has the same first-mount seal as BASE — were they taken before the effect landed, or does the capture harness stub the store? Not determinable read-only.
- How many of the 138 tree-personal findings (studio/**/*.cast, Codex-owned) remain after the merge train's sanitized casts — the release gate stays red until that count is 0, and studio/** is read-only for this order.
- Is ~/timmy/providers.json (0600, may hold OpenRouter/Anthropic keys) acceptable under 'writes only to ~/timmy', given the privacy 0 requirement and that loadConfig reads the OpenRouter key from it (docs/BLANK-SLATE.md:46)?

## VISUAL LANGUAGE

The TUI has ONE hex palette (BASE src/tui/theme.ts, 13 hex literals, "Clearinghouse": navy ground, cyan accent, green seal, amber warn, red danger, violet ident) and a vitest gate that bans hex outside theme.ts. That gate is green in BASE (12/12) and is real: 0 hex, 0 ink named colours, 0 ANSI colour escapes outside theme.ts in BASE, UINEXT2 and C6; the only non-token colour is chalk.gray in ProgressBar.tsx:20, which the gate does not catch. But the palette is the wrong palette for the ORDER: lanes/visual/tokens.json (black/white/grey + SEAL #33FF66 / REFUSE #FF3B3B / PREDICT #FFB020 / GENERATED #8B5CF6, Avenir, uppercase labels, five evidence states) exists only on order/showpiece-spline-s8v2 and order/hana-h2m6; origin/main carries design/tokens.json (navy/phosphor/orange) instead, and no code reads either file. Colour is chosen in 9 modules outside theme.ts (DIM in ShellV2, StatusGlyph, Pill, ActionCards, EscrowReceiptsView, LogRelay, ergonomics, text.ts, color-contract). The doctrine "seal = cryptographic truth ONLY" is broken in code (active tab, NORMAL badge, fleet connected, HANDS HOLD, selected rows all green). No evidence state (declared/constructed/checked/inferred/stale) is rendered anywhere in any tree; no column grid exists (width math is narrow<100 → one 74-col column + rail; rows.ts is vertical only).

**Open questions**

- Which tokens.json is law: showpiece 2026-09-12.3 (has material.evidenceCarrier) or hana 2026-09-12? They differ only in that block; neither is on main.
- Under the ORDER, what happens to today's `warn` uses (running, queued, needs-you, INSERT/CHAT mode badge, cost figures)? PREDICT amber is for predictions only; if these become white/bold, the colour-contract's 'exactly one orange on HOME' rule needs a new definition.
- Does cyan `accent` (focus border, selected row, active tab, cursor, links, ProgressBar/Sparkline fill) collapse to white+bold/inverse, or is a non-semantic interaction hue tolerated? The ORDER text ('accents only with meaning') implies it must go.
- Is design/tokens.json (navy/phosphor product mode + paper mode) retired, or kept for the vault-custody paper surfaces while the TUI moves to lanes/visual/tokens.json?
- Who computes 'stale' for a TUI row (a receipt row whose chain has moved since verification, a DEMOS replay whose cast hash changed)? No data source carries that today.
- Should Metric labels stay lowercase (DESIGN.md §2.4/§5.4) while category headers go uppercase, or does the ORDER's 'uppercase category labels' cover field labels too?
- Does 'one theme module' allow the CHAT dim-under palette (ShellV2 DIM) as a derived export from theme.ts, or must dimming become a dimColor prop?

## ROUTES A (ask the scene · predict-build-judge · render a world · refuse)

None of the four routes is reachable as a route from the TUI keyboard in BASE, UINEXT2 or C6. (1) ASK THE SCENE exists only as a STUDIO preflight seal (demo.ask-the-scene.preflight rc_mu0r538q_9y77, status failed, route_not_connected) plus two CLI-only cousins: `timmy inspect <question>` on origin/order/spatial-t5k1 and `timmy vision spatial …` on origin/order/ctx-c3w8; no /spatial slash command, no RUN join, no DEMOS row, no cast. (2) PREDICT-BUILD-JUDGE is richly proven on the chain (assets.prediction/assets.checkpoint pair, 9 spline.predict/8 spline.checkpoint, hana.prediction/c1/c2, omma.prediction, world.prediction) and has frv2 five-moment captures + ui.captures seal, but the TUI shows only PRED/EV seal ids side by side (UINEXT2 DEMOS) with no difference, no typed views for any of those kinds, and no judgment screen. (3) RENDER A WORLD is real on order/unreal-m3r8 (3 unreal.render seals, unreal.passes, C4/C5 hashes match the chain) with no CLI verb and no TUI surface in BASE; UINEXT2's unreal row/DEMOS fills are wrong-world and its typed view prints dashes. (4) REFUSE has the strongest TUI surface (RUN [r] overlay, REFUSED red rows, §14/privacy/factory refusals on chain), but the human escrow refusal seals status ok, so it never paints red, and the reason never reaches a row. No demo.<name> seals, no `timmy demo run|list`, no replay, no arsenal verb.

**Open questions**

- Which definition of 'ask the scene' is canonical for the ORDER: the STUDIO demo-d6t2 route 1 (`/spatial query` → local model + tools → verifier, untracked studio/demo-d6t2/C0) or spatial-t5k1's `timmy inspect <question>` over a signed SceneIR? Both exist; neither is in main.
- Is a signed receipt body without a receipt.sealed bus echo (unreal.render ×3, hana.*, instrument.ready, privacy.*) considered sealed for §14 cite-exists and for DEMOS fills? src/utils/receipts.ts:223 suggests the echo is informational only.
- Should unreal.render seals carry a prediction_seal/prediction_receipt so the DEMOS PRED column can fill (every current unreal.render has none)?
- Is the repeated frozen-clock demo id rc_mjuohs00_annv (6 bodies at ts 2026-01-01) on the pinned chain an accepted fixture or a leak that a future demo.<name> registry must exclude?
- Which world is THE 'render a world' demo — world-01 observatory, Grand Canyon, or Las Vegas Strip? Only two have home four-worlds folders and the three unreal.render seals point at three different stages.
- Will vision-v7r3's C0 arsenal_debt be recomputed now that unreal.passes exists, and where is arsenal debt meant to be read from live (roster JSON vs chain)?
- For the refuse route, is the intended chain evidence the escrow transition (currently status ok) or a new refusal receipt kind with status denied and a reason field, as recipe.refused already does?

## ROUTES B (eight hands · materialize · blank slate · factory day)

Of the four routes, only EIGHT HANDS has a keyboard path today: BASE (main+#64) reaches HANDS via 6→h→arrows→Enter, but only when .timmy/private/cockpit/board.json exists, the board is the SYNTHETIC lanes/demos/hands-8.rounds.md fixture (real board never taken), [s] opens SEAL-confirm not a send (verified render), and the film shot (`timmy cockpit shot`, cockpit.shot rc_mtykkevp_iskk) lives only on C6/PR #59. Same-commit replay is byte-identical (2/2 takes in C6) but the sealed hashes were taken at 864d39e and do not match tip 386f99d — STALE by the ORDER's own C4 rule; no demo.<name> seal exists for any of the four routes. MATERIALIZE is undefined: the word appears only as spatial-t5k1's proposed SceneIR arrow ("materialization stays proposed; no arrow grants execution"), hana-h2m6's held "C3 materialize arrow", and a swarm-launch flash "panes materialized"; zero chain seals mention it; no TUI surface. BLANK SLATE exists as PR #48 (init wizard, release check, hashed identity) plus a second, incompatible ink wizard on #47; neither is on main; both release.check seals record ok:false with 138 tree findings. FACTORY DAY is undefined: PR #54's omma.build lane (stub takes only, documented transport fails closed, no TUI, colliding `timmy cockpit` verb and hands.json model), the doc-only factory.item roster kind, or the Signal's "12-round authored day" (HOLD at checkpoint 0 step b; RUN SIGNAL panel on #47 only; DEMOS `signal` family can never fill from the chain).

**Open questions**

- Which meaning of 'materialize' does the ORDER intend — the SceneIR proposal→native-part arrow (spatial/Hana), the factory hana.frame finish, or tmux 'panes materialized'? Nothing in any ref or seal decides it.
- Is 'factory day' the factory.item lane run end-to-end, or the Signal's 12-round authored day? FILM-PLAN-v2's Signal handoff says stages c–e must not be reported as started, which would make the Signal reading un-filmable today.
- Should the eight-hands route ever show a real board (the film shot is a labeled SYNTHETIC fixture; ORDER acceptance says 'no personal data on any first screen', a real board carries worktree paths)?
- Which `timmy cockpit` verb set and which registry (board.json vs hands.json) survive when #54 and #64/#59 both land?
- Which `timmy init` wizard is canonical — #48's readline init (identity/seed/providers/project → ~/timmy) or #47's ink WizardScreen (store/operator/edge/commander/policy → .timmy/private/config.json)?
- Do CHAIN typed views exist on BASE/UINEXT2 for cockpit.shot, factory.c2b, omma.prediction, spatial.scene/prediction kinds (C3 scope; not verified in this area)?
- The C6 seal claims 'four takes byte-identical' at 864d39e; the sealed hashes differ from a take at tip 386f99d — is the intended STALE rule per-commit (source_commit in the manifest) or per-tree?

## GATES

BASE (231b0f4): tsc --noEmit exit 0, tsgo (typecheck:native) exit 0, full vitest 93 files/505 tests passed, 5 files/15 tests skipped; typecheck:worker exit 1 (TS2688, workers/ai-proxy deps not installed — the "~86 errors" figure is not reproduced here). CI gate = tsc + tsgo + vitest minus 10 KNOWN_WIP_TESTS; locally 7 of the 10 WIP files pass (34 tests), keyboard-contract.pty and vision-templates skip, ai-proxy is excluded by vitest.config and finds no tests. "keyboard-contract green" is NOT a real signal today: tests/keyboard-contract.pty.test.ts skips unless TIMMY_PTY_TESTS=1 (no node-pty anywhere; it drives tmux), and its assertions are v1-shell markers (MODE:INPUT:COMMAND, SLATE DAG, COMMAND POST, views 5-9) that ShellV2 never renders, while cli.tsx boots ShellV2 by default — the only green keyboard contract is the 6-test reducer suite tests/tui-keyboard.test.ts. Privacy: scan.mjs fixture (negative control) trips 17 gated findings; BASE src/tui, tests, lanes/demos, docs scan 0 findings at medium+ (only review-tier media/brand hits). All 52 UI2 capture frames scan 0 findings at every severity, so "privacy 0 on first screens" holds — but only via an ad-hoc scanText loop: scan.mjs --tree over .timmy/captures returns files:0 because .timmy/ is gitignored and treeFiles uses git ls-files. Pre-commit (core.hooksPath=.githooks in the main repo config) runs scan --staged --fail-on medium and would block a staged home path (pii.home_path=high) unless the blob is byte-identical to origin/main. UINEXT2/C6 carry an older CI (no KNOWN_WIP list, full vitest) and an older scan.mjs; UINEXT2 tests/ui-next.test.ts:88 is a stale assertion contradicted by its own negative-control test. No tokens.json-based visual gate exists on main; design-contract pins the navy/phosphor 13-token theme.

**Open questions**

- Where does the '~86 errors' figure for typecheck:worker on main come from? In this checkout it fails on TS2688 (missing @cloudflare/workers-types) before any program errors; measuring it needs `npm ci` inside workers/ai-proxy, which installs into the symlinked UI2 node_modules tree — not done in a read-only audit.
- The full BASE run reports 15 skipped tests but the five skipIf'd files sum to 14 it() cases by grep; which parameterised/nested case accounts for the 15th?
- gates-uinext2.txt records 'vitest exit=0' after 1 failed file — was the exit code captured through a pipe (masked), and are other pre-measured exit codes in the scratchpad trustworthy?
- Is TIMMY_PTY_TESTS=1 ever set on any runner or operator session, i.e. has tests/keyboard-contract.pty.test.ts executed since ShellV2 became the default (cli.tsx:200)? Not run here because it would launch the live TUI inside the BASE worktree.
- Should the capture privacy gate scan .timmy/captures via a filesystem walk (bypassing git ls-files), and should the ui.captures seal carry the scan's patterns_sha256 + zero-count so §14 CITE-EXISTS can be checked from the chain? (Design decision — outside C0 scope, flagged only.)
- Local gate runs used Node v26.6.0; CI pins Node 24. Any Node-version-dependent behaviour (DEP0190 warnings appear in the vitest log) has not been isolated.

## LANDING PATH

The precondition is currently unsatisfiable: origin/main (01cd3a7) contains neither #47 nor the cockpit view (#64). #64 (2 commits, base = Merge #60) merges cleanly (BASE 231b0f4). #46 (2 commits, base integration/train) and #47 (3 commits, base #46) cannot be merged as-is: retargeting them at main drags in 243 diverged train files and the privacy history scan BLOCKS with 2162 gated findings, whereas the 5 ui-next commits themselves gate 0 (90 review-level only). #47 alone does not apply at all (needs #46's src/harness/uinext.ts). Measured surface of one combined #46+#47 patch on main+#64: 19 files, only 2 conflict — src/tui/components/ShellV2.tsx 9 hunks / 172 conflict lines (every hunk is a C5 row-plan region or a pane signature that C5 gave compact/maxRows) and src/tui/shell-mode.ts 1 hunk / 2 lines (initialShell union). #59/C6 must drop its re-applied C1 commit (864d39e, different patch-id from #64's C1) and cherry-pick only 386f99d: 1 hunk / 7 lines, no #47 dependency. Two `init` verbs (#47 ink wizard vs #48 runInit) collide; ORDER C7 wording matches #48. KNOWN_WIP claim "warroom2 clears with #47" is contradicted locally (passes on main, BASE, UINEXT2; #47 does not touch it). The ui-next tests never existed on main. Minimal sequence: #64 → one ui-next-2-clean commit (supersedes #46+#47) → #59 as a single cherry-pick → resolve init before #48.

**Open questions**

- Which `init` verb is the ORDER's C7 wizard — #47's ink wizard (src/tui/wizard.tsx: store/operator/edge/commander/policy) or #48's runInit (operator, seed, providers, first project → ~/timmy + .timmy/private)? The ORDER text matches #48's semantics but names 'the wizard' (#47) as keep-and-improve.
- Should the recut be ONE commit superseding #46+#47 together (merge-train convention; #47 cannot stand alone) or two stacked -clean commits? One is the minimal path: order/ui-next-2-clean = main(+#64) + integration/train...origin/order/ui-next-2.
- Does tests/warroom2.test.ts actually fail on the ubuntu runner (the only place the KNOWN_WIP claim could hold)? Locally it passes on main, BASE and UINEXT2 and #47 does not touch it.
- Does the ORDER's 'privacy 0' count review-severity findings? The ui-next commits carry 90 review-level media.personal rows (gated 0 at --fail-on medium).
- Is C4 INGEST (6a5c9aa, only on local order/ui-cockpit-k7m3-c5) meant to land for C5's cell states, or is it superseded by the dictated C3 SEND spec?
- Should tests/ui-next.test.ts's failing signal-state case be fixed inside the recut or admitted as KNOWN_WIP (§12 negative control: it is a demonstrated FAIL)?
- After #64 lands, who retires receipt-hotfix and vision-templates from KNOWN_WIP_TESTS (BASE full vitest is green with them)?
- Estimated conflict surface for the whole sequence: #64 → 0; ui-next-2-clean → ShellV2.tsx 9 hunks/172 lines + shell-mode.ts 1 hunk/2 lines (plus the un-measured work of adding three StackSpec entries); #59 cherry-pick → ShellV2.tsx 1 hunk/7 lines. Is that acceptable as the C0 landing estimate, or should the row-plan integration be its own checkpoint?
